","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-26T13:02:43Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2609_29496.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究 VLM 生成解释时，解释到底是在影响答案，还是只是在答案之后做合理化。作者用一个很小的干预来测量这个问题：让模型先生成 rationale 再给答案，或先给答案再生成 rationale，并观察答案、解释和一致性是否发生系统性变化。","importance":"对预训练方向的直接贡献有限，更接近 VLM reasoning evaluation 和解释忠实性诊断。它的启发点在于：生成顺序本身就是一种轻量干预，可以暴露模型是否真的把中间文本作为条件信息使用。这个想法可以迁移到 LLM/VLM 的 reasoning trace、rationale supervision、CoT 数据构造和 post-training 评估中。尤其是在训练数据里同时监督答案和解释时，顺序会改变自回归条件结构；rationale-first 让答案条件化在解释上，answer-first 则更容易训练出事后解释。","technical_approach":"方法包括两个部分。第一是 generation-order intervention：在保持任务、图像、问题、指令格式基本一致的情况下，对比 rationale-first 和 answer-first。RF 设置要求模型先给短解释，再输出答案；AF 设置要求模型先输出答案，再给简短理由。两者都用结构化 JSON 输出，便于解析。第二是 explanation-supervised fine-tuning：作者构造带解释的训练样本，并为同一条样本建立两种目标序列 R*→A* 和 A*→R*。解释 R* 是在给定真实答案 A* 的条件下后验构造的，目的是降低错误解释噪声，并确保解释与正确答案一致。训练目标仍是标准自回归似然，只是同时覆盖两种输出顺序。","experiments":"实验使用 7B 级 VLM，包括 Qwen-2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 和 LLaVA-1.5-7B。作者认为 Qwen-VL 在初步 zero-shot 中推理能力更强，因此用它为 explanation-augmented fine-tuning 生成 rationale。任务覆盖三类视觉语言推理数据，包含知识密集型 VQA、视觉 grounding 和需要外部常识或世界知识的推理场景。可见内容没有给出完整数值结果，因此不应过度解读提升幅度。实验要点是比较 zero-shot 与 fine-tuning 下 RF/AF 的准确率、解释一致性和输出结构稳定性。如果 RF 与 AF 的结果差异显著，说明解释并非稳定反映同一决策过程；如果 rationale supervision 后差异缩小，说明训练可以部分校准解释与答案之间的条件关系。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 15–25 分钟。重点看 Method 中的 generation-order intervention 和双顺序监督目标，这部分是主要方法贡献。实验部分可快速查看不同模型、不同任务、zero-shot 与 fine-tuning 设置下 RF/AF 的差异。对预训练研究员而言，最值得关注的不是具体 VQA 分数，而是“输出顺序改变自回归因果结构”这一点。它对设计 reasoning 数据格式有现实意义：如果希望解释影响答案，应优先考虑 rationale-first 或显式中间状态条件化；如果只需要可读 justification，answer-first 更便宜但忠实性更弱。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether explanations generated by vision-language models actually affect predictions or merely rationalize answers after the fact. It introduces a simple intervention: generate the rationale before the answer, or generate the answer before the rationale, while keeping the task and prompt structure controlled.","importance":"The work is mainly an evaluation and diagnostic study for VLM reasoning faithfulness, with indirect relevance to pretraining and post-training. The useful idea is that generation order changes the autoregressive conditioning structure. In rationale-first generation, the answer can condition on the explanation. In answer-first generation, the explanation is more likely to be a post-hoc justification. This matters for rationale supervision, CoT-style data construction, and multimodal instruction tuning.","technical_approach":"The method has two components. The first is a generation-order intervention comparing rationale-first and answer-first prompting under a shared structured-output format, implemented with JSON for reliable parsing. The second is explanation-supervised fine-tuning. For each example, the authors construct two target sequences using the same answer and rationale: R*→A* and A*→R*. The rationales are produced post hoc while conditioning on the ground-truth answer, so the supervision is answer-consistent and less noisy than free-form intermediate reasoning. Training uses the standard autoregressive likelihood over both sequence orders.","experiments":"The experiments use 7B-scale VLMs: Qwen-2.5-VL-7B-Instruct and LLaVA-1.5-7B. Qwen-VL is treated as the stronger model in preliminary zero-shot tests and is used to generate rationales for explanation-augmented fine-tuning. The evaluation covers three visual-language reasoning datasets spanning knowledge-intensive VQA, visual grounding, and world-knowledge inference. The provided text does not expose the full numerical tables, so the safe summary is methodological: the key measurements are accuracy, consistency, and output stability under RF versus AF, in both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings. Large RF/AF gaps indicate that explanations are not causally stable parts of the prediction process.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 15–25 minutes. Read the method section carefully, especially the generation-order intervention and the dual-order fine-tuning objective. The experimental section is useful mainly for seeing whether RF/AF gaps persist across models and datasets. For pretraining-oriented readers, the main lesson is about data format: if an explanation is meant to guide the answer, it should appear before the answer or be represented as an explicit conditioning variable; if it appears after the answer, it is much easier for the model to learn fluent justification without faithful reasoning.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"可看实验设计而非分数；它提醒 rationale 数据不等于推理监督，尤其别默认 answer-first 解释是干净信号。","en":"Read the intervention setup, not the headline scores; it is a useful warning that rationale data is not automatically clean reasoning supervision."},"metadata":{"institutions":["German Research Center for Artificial Intelligence","University of Oldenburg","Affiliation:German Research Center for Artificial Intelligence","Affiliation:University of Oldenburg"],"authors":["Siting Liang","Luca Rippe","Omar Adjali","Daniel Sonntag"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["VLM","rationale","generation-order","reasoning-eval"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29496","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.29496","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.29933","title":"An Empirical Study of VLM Pipelines for Long-Document QA","abstract":"Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used for long-document processing, where the inputs combine text with charts, tables, figures, and complex layouts. lexical、dense-text、vision late-interaction 和 rerank 系列。agent 方案包括 function-calling、gist-based 和 iterative-refinement。核心 agent 使用 MinerU 解析 PDF，抽取章节、页面文本块、图表裁剪和结构化 catalog；VLM PDF to the model, which retriever to use when only a subset of pages can be passed, and whether the model should operate as an agent that calls page, table, figure, and search tools. It is an empirical pipeline study rather than a new VLM pretraining method.","importance":"The main value for foundation-model research is diagnostic. Long-document multimodal QA is not determined by context length alone. Accuracy also depends on page retrieval, layout parsing, access to tables and figures, and the model’s ability to use tools reliably. The results show that agentic document reading is model-capability dependent: smaller readers may lose to static inputs, while stronger readers can turn structured catalogs and tool calls into better answers. This points to training gaps around document navigation, evidence localization, and multi-turn tool use.","technical_approach":"The study decomposes a document QA system into three axes: input modality, retriever, and agentic policy. Input modes include raw PDFs, all-page images, plain text, layout-aware text, and top-k retrieved page images. Retrieval methods cover lexical retrieval, dense-text retrieval, vision late interaction, and reranking. Agent variants include function-calling, gist-based, and iterative-refinement designs. The main function-calling agent uses MinerU to parse each PDF into a structural catalog containing section information, page ranges, page text blocks, and figure/table crops. The VLM receives this compact catalog and decides which tools to call. Evaluation is standardized: free-form answers are first normalized into short answers by Claude Haiku 4.5, then scored with each benchmark’s official deterministic scorer.","experiments":"The evaluation uses MMLongBench-Doc and LongDocURL with Claude Sonnet 4.5 through an API and local Qwen3.5-4B, 9B, and 27B readers. With Sonnet 4.5, the agent improves over the same-reader top-5 retrieval pipeline by 11.7 percentage points. On LongDocURL, the agent is at least tied with or ahead of static input across readers and is level with the best retrieval pipeline. MLLM 在视觉推理中“看错地方”或“不看图”的问题。它不直接监督推理链，而是尝试约束文本 token 到图像 token 的注意力，让模型在回答时把更多注意力质量放到与问题相关的视觉区域。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在 VLM 训练信号设计。它把视觉 grounding 做成一种弱监督的注意力正则，而不是依赖密集标注或额外推理数据。这个方向适合作为视觉-语言继续预训练、instruction tuning 或视觉推理增强阶段的轻量训练信号。更有用的一点是，论文强调不是所有 attention head 都应被同样约束，视觉 grounding 能力集中在少数 head 或问题上下文中抽取带属性的名词短语，例如颜色、位置、大小等，再用分割模型得到相应区域。训练时不要求 attention map 与 mask 做逐 patch 匹配，只要求 mask 内的总 attention mass 增加。这样可以容忍 mask 偏大或边界不准，因为目标只约束区域内总量，不规定区域内每个视觉 token 的精确分布。另一部分是 Adaptive Head Selection。作者先分析各层各 head 的视觉参与度和空间 grounding 行为，只对 grounding 响应强的 head 加 sPMC loss，其余 head 不约束。这个设计区别于全 head attention alignment：它承认 Transformer head 有功能分化，避免把语言建模、格式控制或其他功能 head mask 的噪声特性。实验还支持一个内部机制判断：强视觉参与和空间 grounding 并不均匀分布在所有 head 中，而是集中在一小部分注意力路径上。","reading_guide":"建议泛读方法和消融部分，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看三处：第一，mask 构造流程，尤其是 noun phrase 提取和分割先验如何进入训练；第二，Adaptive Head Selection relies on language priors without using the necessary visual evidence. The goal is not to supervise the reasoning trace directly, but to improve implicit visual grounding through attention regularization.","importance":"The useful signal for pretraining-oriented work is the design of a weak grounding objective for VLM training. Instead of requiring dense attention labels or reasoning supervision, the method uses coarse segmentation-derived masks and turns them into a lightweight training loss. The more interesting observation is head specialization: visual grounding is concentrated in a small subset of attention heads, and regularizing only those heads works better than applying the same constraint everywhere.","technical_approach":"The proposed method, Selective Probability Mass Concentration, encourages selected text-to-image attention heads to allocate more total probability mass inside a relevant visual region. The mask is produced from external segmentation priors: noun phrases with modifiers are extracted from captions or input context, then used to obtain spatial masks. The loss does not force a token-by-token attention pattern. It only increases aggregate in-mask mass, which makes it more tolerant to noisy or over-inclusive masks. Adaptive Head Selection identifies heads with strong visual engagement and spatial grounding behavior, then applies the sPMC objective only to those heads. This differs from dense attention alignment and all-head regularization, both of which assume that all heads should follow the same visual supervision.","experiments":"The paper evaluates the method on multiple MLLMs and six multimodal benchmark suites. When applied to hyperbolic multimodal models, they do not explicitly preserve the Lorentz geometry that jointly encodes within-modality similarity, cross-modal correspondence, and semantic hierarchy; sequential updates can therefore retain task scores while still distorting previously learned relations. plan","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-24T17:48:19Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2609_30221.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是一般意义上的“提示词润色”，而是把 text-to-video 前端的 prompt enhancement 重新定义成一项接近分镜与导演规划的任务：给定用户一句较短、较粗的需求，模型需要生成一个跨镜头、跨时间、跨要素一致的 cinematic plan，里面不仅有主体和动作，还要包含镜头调度、光线、音效、对白、事件顺序与 shot transition。作者指出，传统 forward rewriting 的监督方式来自“把用户请求扩写成更长描述”的合成分布，这和下游视频生成器真正见过的、由真实视频反推出来的 video-grounded caption 分布并不一致；当视频时长拉到 30 秒、条件复杂度上升后，这种分布错配会直接表现为镜头间不连贯、语义遗漏、角色绑定错误和时序冲突。论文的核心问题因此有两个：一是如何学到更贴近真实成片结构的 cinematic planning；二是如何在长计划中持续保留用户约束，不让需求在后续 shot 中漂移或丢失。","importance":"对基座研究的直接价值不在于视频生成本身，而在于它把“条件生成器”当成一个独立的大模型建模问题来做，并且给出了两个对预训练/后训练接口都很有启发的设计。第一，监督信号不再来自人工改写，而是来自真实视频分布上的 reverse construction，这和语言模型里越来越常见的“从结果反推训练目标”思路相通，属于数据构造范式上的变化。第二，作者把长程语义保持问题显式写进 RL 奖励，用九维 reward 去约束遗漏、错绑、时序不一致等错误，这和长上下文 instruction following、agent 轨迹一致性、structured generation 的 reward design 有明显共性。量级上，这不是通用 LLM 预训练论文，但它提供了一个较完整的 recipe：如何把真实世界多模态产物转成层级文本计划，再用 SFT+GRPO 去训练一个大规模条件规划器。对做多模态统一建模、视频/语音前端 planning、以及 post-train reward 设计的人有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法上最关键的是“reverse SFT + semantic-consistency RL”这条训练链路。作者先形式化 T2V 系统为 prompt enhancer πθ 与 video generator Gϕ 的组合，目标不是直接生成视频，而是生成更适合 Gϕ 消化的文本条件 y。与常见做法从用户请求 x 正向扩写到 y 不同，WanPE 从高质量真实视频 v 出发，先抽取一个层级化 cinematic condition y，再反向重建一个与之兼容、但信息更稀疏的用户请求 x，用 (x, y) 做监督。这样做的理由很明确：y 来自真实视频，天然落在下游生成器更熟悉的 video-grounded 条件分布上；x 则模拟真实用户输入，形成更接近推理时的输入输出关系。\n\n从文中描述看，这个 hierarchical cinematic condition 不是单段长文本，而是按 shot 组织的结构化计划，覆盖场景、主体、动作、镜头语言、光照、音频/对白以及跨 shot 的叙事推进。它解决的是多因素耦合问题：动作变化会影响镜头运动，镜头运动又会影响节奏和转场，不能像普通 prompt rewrite 那样只做局部修饰。\n\n在 SFT 之后，作者引入 SC-GRPO。这里的重点不是 GRPO 本身，而是 reward 的设计。论文给了一个九维 semantic consistency reward，用来检查用户需求是否在整个计划中被完整保留，尤其关注四类错误：需求遗漏、语义篡改、实体/属性/动作绑定错误、时间顺序和跨镜头一致性冲突。相比只看最终文本质量或流畅度，这种 reward 更接近 structured fidelity。它的作用类似在长序列生成里加入一个全局 verifier，让模型在扩写时不只是“写得像导演”，还要“始终忠于原需求”。\n\n另外一个值得注意的点是可迁移性。作者强调 WanPE 经过 format adaptation 后可以迁移到不同视频生成器上，说明它学习的不是某个单一 generator 的私有 prompt 模板，而是更抽象的 cinematic planning 表达。这一点决定了它更像一个前端 planning model，而不是某个闭源系统里的 prompt 工具。","experiments":"实验部分最有信息量的是三类结果。第一类是规模与总体收益。作者训练了 4B 到 397B 的多个 WanPE 版本，数据来自 1.05M 真实视频。按摘要和引言给出的主结果，WanPE-397B 相比 raw prompt，在 5–15 秒视频上最高提升 18.84 分，在 30 秒视频上最高提升 50.86 分。这个差距说明随着时长增加，prompt enhancer 的价值不是线性增长，而是因为长视频更依赖前端规划，收益会被放大。\n\n第二类是方法消融。reverse construction 明确优于 forward rewriting，说明训练分布是否贴近真实视频条件，比单纯把 prompt 写得更华丽更重要。SC-GRPO 在不同模型规模上带来 18.6 到 23.3 分的 semantic consistency 提升，这个幅度不小，说明长计划里的主要瓶颈确实是需求保持，而不是语言表面质量。若全文表格中区分了 omission、binding、temporal consistency 等子项，建议重点看这些 reward 维度分别改善了什么，因为这能帮助判断 RL 是在修复哪类错误。\n\n第三类是评测设计。作者构建了 WanPEval，一个覆盖 5 到 30 秒、不同 intent granularity 的人工标注测试集，并做了文本级评分与约 11K 次盲测视频两两比较。这个设计比只看文本评估更扎实，因为 prompt enhancer 的最终价值还是要通过视频体现。文中还提到 WanPE 在 5–15 秒上领先所评估的商业方案，在 30 秒上与 Seedance 2.5 竞争接近；这说明方法在中短时长上更成熟，长时长则仍受下游 generator 能力上限约束。若全文给出不同 generator 上的 transfer 结果，建议特别看 format adaptation 前后差异，这能判断它到底学到了多少“通用 planning”而不是“平台特定提示模板”。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。第一看 Introduction 里对 forward rewriting 与 reverse construction prompt polishing. It reframes prompt enhancement for text-to-video as a planning problem: given a short user request, generate a shot-structured cinematic plan that stays coherent over time and preserves user intent across subjects, actions, camera motion, lighting, dialogue, audio, and event order. The authors argue that standard forward prompt rewriting is trained on a synthetic rewrite distribution, while downstream video generators are conditioned on captions grounded in real videos. As generation length reaches 30 seconds, this mismatch becomes a real modeling issue rather than a stylistic one. The resulting failures are omissions, broken subject-action bindings, temporal contradictions, and poor cross-shot coherence. The paper therefore targets two coupled problems: learning a planning distribution closer to real filmed structure, and maintaining semantic fidelity throughout a long multi-shot plan.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research comes from the training formulation rather than the video application itself. The work treats the condition generator as a standalone large-model problem and proposes a full recipe built around data construction and post-training rewards. First, supervision is obtained through reverse construction from real videos instead of synthetic forward rewriting. That is a meaningful shift in how training targets are defined. Second, long-range semantic preservation is made explicit in RL through a multi-dimensional reward that penalizes omissions, altered constraints, wrong bindings, and temporal inconsistency. This is closely related to issues in long-context instruction following, structured generation, and agent trajectory consistency. The contribution is not a core LLM pretraining result, but it is a useful case study for multimodal planning models, data-grounded supervision design, and reward shaping for long-form conditional generation.","technical_approach":"$29","experiments":"$2a","reading_guide":"$2b","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"看 SC-GRPO 的奖励定义和 WanPEval；这类规划器会影响视频生成数据与偏好闭环，但别把它当基座预训练论文。","en":"Read the SC-GRPO reward and WanPEval setup; this informs the planner-generator feedback loop, but it is not a base pretraining paper."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yubo Zhu","Yawen Shao","Ziyun Dai","Zixun Fang","Kai Zhu","Siyang Sun","Haolan Xue","Chuxin Wang","Tingyu Weng","Jingming Luo","Chen Shi","Lianghua Huang","Yufeng Ai","Yuzheng Wang","Wenyuan Zhang","Yu Shang","Yuxiang Bao","Zoubin Bi","Jie Xiao","Jinbo Xing","Jiaxing Zhao","Chongyang Zhong","Hengjian Chen","Chenwei Xie","Akide Liu","Zhehan Kan","Yu Liu","Wei Zhai","Sheng Zhong","Wei Tong"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["text-to-video","prompt-enhancement","GRPO","video-generation","cinematic-planning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30221","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.30221","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.29116","title":"Spectral Amplitude Purification in Distribution Matching for Diffusion Distillation","abstract":"Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) enables high-quality diffusion sampling in only a few steps, but its optimization dynamics remain dominated by coarse, low-frequency signals, delaying the recovery of fine-grained details. We identify a pronounced concentration of spectral amplitudes at low frequencies in the DMD directional error, where dominant low-frequency components overwhelm weaker mid- and high-frequency signals. for","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-24T06:49:38Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2609_29116.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"DMD 类少步扩散蒸馏在训练早期更容易学到低频粗结构，细纹理和清晰边界恢复较慢；核心原因被定位为 DMD directional error 的频谱幅值强烈集中在低频，导致优化更新被少数大幅值低频分量主导。","importance":"这篇工作的价值不在于提出新的生成模型主干，而在于给 DMD 训练动态提供了一个可操作的频域诊断。对预训练和蒸馏研究有两点启发：第一，蒸馏信号的不同频段可能存在严重的梯度预算不均衡，平均 loss 看不出这个问题；第二，可以在不改教师模型、不改推理图的情况下，对训练中的方向场做结构化调制，从而改变收敛路径。这个思路也适合迁移到 video、audio diffusion 或统一多模态生成模型的蒸馏阶段。","technical_approach":"方法建立在 Distribution Matching Distillation 上。DMD 通过最小化 few-step generator 边缘分布和预训练扩散模型边缘分布之间的时间加权 reverse KL 来训练生成器。生成器更新由 real score 与 fake score 的差值驱动，其中 real score 来自冻结教师模型，fake score 来自辅助 fake model，并通过 denoising score matching 训练。SAP-DMD 不改变这个框架，而是在 DMD directional field 上做二维 FFT，分析其幅值谱和相位谱。它保留相位，只对幅值谱中异常大的上尾部分做自适应抑制，再反变换回空间域作为更新信号。这样做的取舍很明确：如果直接增强高频，容易引入噪声和不稳定；如果完全重写 loss，会改变 DMD 的固定点。SAP-DMD 选择削弱过强低频幅值，让中高频修正更容易进入有效更新，同时尽量保持原始方向场的几何结构。","experiments":"实验覆盖 PixArt-α、SD3 和 SD3.5 等 text-to-image 模型，并在 2-step 与 4-step 采样设置下验证。结果显示，SAP-DMD 在训练收敛速度和生成质量上均优于普通 DMD 及竞争性少步蒸馏基线，尤其体现在纹理、边缘和局部细节恢复上。文中强调该方法只在训练期引入轻量 FFT 计算，推理阶段不增加网络调用次数，也不改变采样流程。已给出的材料未包含具体 FID、CLIP score 或人评数值，因此不应把结论理解为某个单一指标的大幅提升，而应看作对 DMD 优化信号的一种稳定改造。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看三处：第一，Introduction 中关于 DMD 细节恢复滞后的观察，以及 Figure 1 对粗结构和细节收敛差异的展示；第二，Preliminaries 中 DMD 梯度由 real/fake score 差值构成的推导，这是理解 SAP-DMD 插入位置的关键；第三，方法和消融部分中幅值上尾抑制、相位保持、FFT 开销、2-step/4-step 对比。值得深挖的问题是：这种频域调制是否会在更长视频、音频 spectrogram 或 latent diffusion 的不同 latent 分辨率下保持稳定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a training-dynamics issue in DMD-style few-step diffusion distillation: coarse low-frequency structure is learned earlier than fine textures and sharp boundaries because the DMD directional error is dominated by a small set of large low-frequency spectral amplitudes.","importance":"The contribution is mainly a diagnostic and training-signal intervention for diffusion distillation, rather than a new backbone. It is useful for pretraining researchers because it shows that distillation gradients can have strong spectral imbalance even when the scalar loss looks normal. It also demonstrates a low-intrusion way to reshape the optimization path without changing the teacher, the generator architecture, or inference-time sampling. The same type of analysis may be relevant to video, audio, and unified multimodal diffusion distillation.","technical_approach":"The method builds on Distribution Matching Distillation. DMD trains a few-step generator by minimizing a time-weighted reverse KL between the generator marginal and the target diffusion marginal. The generator update is driven by the difference between a frozen real score model and a trainable fake score model. SAP-DMD inserts a frequency-domain operation on this directional field. It applies a 2D FFT, adaptively suppresses excessive upper-tail amplitudes in the spectrum, preserves the phase, and transforms the field back before applying the update. The design avoids directly amplifying high frequencies, which could inject noise, and avoids changing the DMD objective or its fixed point. The main idea is to reduce the disproportionate effect of dominant low-frequency components so that mid- and high-frequency corrections receive more effective optimization signal.","experiments":"The experiments cover text-to-image distillation on PixArt-α, SD3, and SD3.5, with both 2-step and 4-step sampling. The reported results indicate faster detail recovery and better few-step generation quality than standard DMD and competitive baselines. The method only adds lightweight FFT computation during training and has no additional inference-time cost. The provided material does not include exact FID, CLIP, or human-evaluation numbers, so the strongest supported conclusion is about improved convergence behavior and qualitative detail recovery under the tested setups.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus on the empirical observation in the introduction, especially the comparison between coarse-structure recovery and fine-detail recovery; the DMD gradient formulation in the preliminaries, which explains where SAP-DMD is inserted; and the method and ablation sections on amplitude-tail suppression, phase preservation, FFT overhead, and 2-step versus 4-step behavior. A useful follow-up question is whether the same spectral purification remains stable for video diffusion, audio spectrogram diffusion, or latent diffusion at different latent resolutions.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"扩散模型蒸馏的频域分析工作，发现DMD误差被低频主导，通过频域滤波加速收敛，对做diffusion加速或连续时间流匹配的同学有一定启发。","en":"Frequency-domain analysis of diffusion distillation, finding DMD error is dominated by low frequencies; the proposed spectral filtering accelerates convergence, offering insights for those working on diffusion acceleration or continuous-time flow matching."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Zhejiang University","University of California, Berkeley","Affiliation:Zhejiang University","Affiliation:University of California, Berkeley"],"authors":["Zhenyu Zhou","Can Wang","Chun Chen","Zeyu Zheng","Defang Chen"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Diffusion Models","Distribution Matching Distillation","Frequency Domain Analysis","Fast Sampling"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29116","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.29116","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.28561","title":"CARE: Condition-Aware Representation Regularization for Diffusion Models","abstract":"Recent advances in diffusion models highlight the importance of representation regularization for improving sample quality and training efficiency. However, commonly used regularization methods often overlook the built-in conditions (such as labels or texts) which directly determine the generation target. In this work, we demonstrate how conditioning signals affect the feature distribution and introduce the CARE (Condition-Aware REpresentation regularization). base checkpoint 稳定地做成可复现的高质量 post-training 流水线。作者关心的核心矛盾有三个。第一，很多公开模型只放结果，不放阶段顺序、数据配比、奖励设计和基础设施细节，外部团队很难复现。第二，多阶段 RL 容易被 reward hacking、训练不稳定和能力回退拖垮，尤其是从 reasoning、coding 到 agent、RLHF 这种奖励可靠性逐步下降的链条。第三，MoE base 模型在后训练里会暴露出真实工程问题，比如 rollout 吞吐、长轨迹稳定性、chat template 一致性和多轮 on-policy 采样，这些往往比算法名词更决定成败。作者给出的答案是：把 post-training 当成一条有明确阶段职责、奖励硬度排序和基础设施约束的串行 recipe，而不是把若干 SFT/RL 技术随意拼接。","importance":"对基座研究的价值主要不在“又做出一个聊天模型”，而在于把 pretrain 之后的能力塑形过程拆成了可讨论、可迁移的训练接口。它有三层意义。第一，它把 SFT 从“RL 前的热身”重新定义成能力地基：后续 RL 主要是在已有能力上做选择性放大和行为校准，而不是从零长出 reasoning、coding 或 agentic 行为。这一点对 pretrain↔posttrain 接口很重要，因为它说明 base model 的可塑性与 SFT 数据质量之间存在强耦合。第二，它把阶段顺序和奖励可靠性绑定起来：先做可验证奖励的 RLVR，再做 judge-based 或 preference-based 优化，目的是缩短模型暴露在可被利用的软奖励下的时间。这对设计长链路 post-training 很有参考价值。第三，它把基础设施视为 recipe 的一部分，而不是实现细节。token-in/token-out rollout、Rollout Routing Replay、大 batch、稳定 sandbox、统一 chat template，这些都直接影响 RL 信号质量。量级上，这不是提出新理论的工作，而是一份高密度的工程方法学总结；对想做开放式 post-training、agent RL 或研究 RL 如何反哺基座能力的人，信息密度很高。","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"文中最关键的实验不是单个 benchmark 分数，而是 stagewise 结果和 recipe 设计的经验结论。已给出的总体结果是：最终 Rufus-Air 在作者报告的公开评测中，除 Arena-Hard v2 Creative Writing 外，整体优于官方 GLM-4.5-Air；并且与同量级开源模型 INTELLECT-3、Nemotron-3-Super 竞争，指令跟随和多数 agent/alignment 项上更强，在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 与 Nemotron-3-Super 打平，Tau2-Airline 和 Tau2-Retail 个别项略落后，创意写作项不占优。\n\n更值得看的是分阶段评估逻辑。作者不是只拿最终模型和外部模型比，而是让每个阶段都和自己的起始 checkpoint 比，目的是看该阶段究竟带来了什么、又牺牲了什么。这个设计很重要，因为多阶段后训练里后续阶段会覆盖前面阶段的参数更新，如果只看最终总分，很难知道收益来自哪里。\n\n从文中结论看，SFT 对后续上限影响很大，Reasoning RL 还能显著帮助后续 IF RL；这说明 reasoning 训练不只是提升数学/逻辑题，而是在改善 instruction retention 和复杂任务分解所需的中间表征。作者还强调 difficulty filtering 是多数 RL 阶段稳定增益的关键：去掉模型已解决和始终失败的样本后，训练更聚焦于可学习区间。虽然当前给出的正文片段没有展开全部表格数字，但已经明确给出工程规模：基于 12B active 的 MoE，RL 阶段可在 8–32 节点完成，计算门槛明显低于 frontier 级闭源流程。若精读全文，应重点提取 Table 2 的阶段增益、各 RL 阶段的 prompt 过滤比例、以及 Table 16 的算力配置，这些比最终榜单更有复用价值。","reading_guide":"建议按“先总后细”的顺序读。\n\n第一遍先看 Introduction 和 Recipe recipe 主干，45 分钟可以读完核心部分。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a practical but under-documented question: how to turn an open base checkpoint into a strong, reproducible post-training stack using only open components, public data, and moderate compute. The real problem is not a single capability such as reasoning or coding. It is how to organize a long sequence of SFT, RLVR, agent RL, and RLHF so that each stage adds capability without collapsing stability, inviting reward hacking, or erasing earlier gains. The authors also treat infrastructure as part of the learning recipe. Based on the theoretical observation that if a new reward is a linear combination of existing ones, the optimal policy is a log-mixture of the base model and existing policies. PoEM estimates composition weights via linear regression on a small set of samples scored under the new reward. and reinforcement learning are usually run as separate stages, which leaves their strengths poorly integrated. Distillation gives dense token-level supervision but can keep the student too close to the teacher. RL optimizes task reward but often causes entropy collapse and unstable optimization. Long-horizon agent tasks further require better credit assignment than a final-answer reward can provide. Pistis asks two concrete questions: can on-policy distillation and RL be interleaved inside one training loop so that they shape each other during optimization, and can intermediate actions in long trajectories be credited more accurately instead of being rewarded simply because the final outcome was successful?","importance":"For a foundation-model researcher, the value of this work lies in the pretrain-to-posttrain interface rather than in the benchmark numbers alone. That matters for post-training stability, policy entropy preservation, and potentially for mid-training or continual adaptation settings. PAS is also relevant beyond this paper because it targets a general issue in long-horizon reasoning and tool use: process quality matters, and trajectory-level success is too coarse a signal. Finally, PAH separates policy learning from inference-harness optimization, which is practically important in agent systems because it prevents system-level gains from being conflated with model-level gains. At the rollout level, hard queries---those with high semantic entropy---frequently produce unanimously wrong sample groups, collapsing the group-relative   advantage to zero exactly where hallucination risk is highest. Revisiting the ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-24T03:20:58Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_28963.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理 agentic RL 中的 step-level credit assignment 问题：GRPO 这类 group-based 方法在 response-level 上合理，但直接把 trajectory-level advantage 分配到多步轨迹中的每一步，会系统性误判单个步骤的贡献。","importance":"它对 post-train 和 RL-for-agent 方向更直接，对预训练研究的启发在训练信号质量。论文指出，GRPO 成功的关键不是简单的 group normalization，而是同一 state 下多个 action 的均值可以作为 state value 的估计。多轮 agent 任务中，不同轨迹在相同步数通常已经处于不同 state，按 step index 做 group normalization 会把不同 state 的样本混在一起，导致 advantage 有偏。这个分析对长链路推理、工具调用、多轮环境交互的 RL 训练很有参考价值。","technical_approach":"论文提出 GRAFT，即 Graph-based Faithful Step-level credit assignment。核心做法是把同一 prompt 下采样得到的多条轨迹从孤立链条重组为一个 trajectory graph。图中的 node 表示 state，edge 表示 action。构图时通过精确匹配或 embedding 相似度匹配来合并语义相同或相近的 state。这样，同一 node 下的 outgoing actions 可以形成真正来自同一 state 的 action group，用于更可信地估计 step-level advantage。随后，方法只利用 terminal outcome reward，在图上做 Bellman value propagation，为每个 node 估计 value。在已有 node value 后，单步 advantage 定义为 A_t = gamma V(s_{t+1}) - V(s_t)，与标准 RL 定义对齐。论文还提出 Graph GAE，把 GAE 扩展到 trajectory graph 上，用多跳后续 advantage 的加权和降低 value 估计偏差和方差。与 PRM 路线相比，GRAFT 不需要 step-level 人工标注或过程奖励模型；与直接在相同步数做归一化相比，它避免了把不同 state 混合作为同一 value baseline 的问题；与从每个中间 state 重新采样大量 continuation 的无偏方案相比，它不增加 O(N^2 T) rollout 成本。","experiments":"全文提供的片段只给出总体结论，没有列出具体 benchmark 名称、模型规模和数值提升。论文声称在多种 multi-turn agentic benchmarks 上，相比 GRPO 和近期 agentic RL 算法取得一致改进。可确认的实验重点应包括三类比较：对原始 GRPO 的 step-level 改造收益；对使用 PRM 或按 step index 做 group normalization 的方法比较；以及 graph construction、Bellman propagation、Graph GAE 的消融。由于可见内容没有具体数字，不能可靠给出提升幅度。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 30 到 40 分钟。优先看 Sec. 2 的 preliminary analysis，因为那里解释了为什么 GRPO 在 response-level 上成立、为什么 step-level 套用会产生系统偏差。然后看 Sec. 3 的 trajectory graph 构造、value propagation 和 Graph GAE，这三部分是方法核心。实验部分建议重点找两类表格：一是多轮 agent benchmark 的主结果，二是 state matching 方式、图传播迭代、GAE 参数的消融。若只关心训练算法，可跳过较长的 benchmark 描述，集中看 advantage 估计公式和复杂度讨论。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses step-level credit assignment in agentic RL. In multi-turn agent tasks, trajectories at the same step index usually correspond to different intermediate states. teacher，再用普通 context 下的 student 去拟合。这会引入不可消除的信息差。提出的方法叫 Privileged Self-Practice。它保留 privileged information，但把 PI 从 loss 移到 sampler：当某个任务当前 rollout 成功率低于阈值时，用 analyzer model 生成一条短的 per-task instruction，把这条 instruction 放进上下文重新采样一组 guided rollouts。guided 和 unguided rollouts 都在各自采样上下文下评分，并在各自组内计算 advantage，然后使用同一个 GRPO 目标训练。关键点是训练目标不比较有 PI 和无 PI 两种视图，因此不会要求学生模仿自己看不到的信息。推理时移除 instruction，模型仍使用普通上下文执行。","experiments":"实验在 AppWorld 和 SWE-bench Verified 上进行，覆盖三个 student model。结果显示 PSP 在所有设置中平均分最高，并且是唯一稳定超过 plain GRPO 的方法。论文报告的最大提升为：AppWorld 的 task-goal completion 最高提升 65%，SWE-bench Verified 的 resolved rate 最高提升 61%。一个重要负例是 OPSD 类方法在多轮 agent 上可能退化到低于未训练 base model，说明 dense imitation signal 不一定比稀疏 outcome reward 更安全；如果 teacher 的优势来自 student 不可见的信息，token-level 蒸馏会把“结论”复制给学生，但不会复制产生结论的证据。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 35–45 分钟。先看 Introduction 对 OPSD 失效机制的解释，重点理解“confidence without information”。再看 Preliminaries 中 multi-turn trajectory 和 GRPO 的定义，随后看 PSP 的 sampler 的案例分析。若时间有限，优先读方法和失败案例，数字结果作为可信度检查即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why on-policy self-distillation with privileged information can fail for multi-turn agents. The teacher view is conditioned on extra information such as reference solutions or hindsight analysis, while the student will not have that information at test time. Token-level distillation teaches the student to take confident actions without the evidence that justified them, and in an interactive environment those actions are executed and affect later states.","importance":"The result is useful for agent post-training and for designing self-training pipelines. The main lesson is that privileged information should not necessarily be used as a token-level imitation target. In multi-turn settings, it is often safer to use it to improve sampling and generate informative rollouts, while keeping the RL objective defined within the context actually used to sample each trajectory. This has implications for synthetic data generation, rejection sampling, self-improvement, and RL training for agents.","technical_approach":"$36","experiments":"The experiments cover AppWorld and SWE-bench Verified with three student models. The challenge is not just to save inference tokens, but to learn routing and response generation jointly under online RL. Three issues make this hard. First, routing supervision is counterfactual and non-stationary: for any prompt, a free rollout only reveals the chosen mode, while the better mode can change as the policy improves. Second, routing and response generation live at different decision levels, so sequence-level RL tends to mix up “bad route” with “bad answer under a good route.” Third, autonomous routing creates self-reinforcing exploration failure: an early preference for one mode suppresses evidence for the other. The paper aims to solve all three within a single shared policy.","importance":"For pretraining-adjacent research, the main value is in how it treats test-time compute as a learned internal control variable rather than an external prompt switch or a separate router. That matters for the broader pretrain-to-posttrain interface: if reasoning budget is trainable, then compute allocation becomes part of the model policy, not just a deployment heuristic. The second contribution is methodological. The paper gives a fairly clean hierarchical RL recipe: use current-policy paired counterfactual rollouts for cross-mode comparison, assign that signal only to the routing token, and keep within-mode GRPO on response tokens. That separation is useful because many reasoning RL setups optimize the whole trajectory with entangled credit. This is not a new pretraining paradigm, but it is a meaningful training design for native dual-mode checkpoints and compute-aware reasoning policies.","technical_approach":"$39","experiments":"$3a","reading_guide":"$3b","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看路由 token 的反事实 credit 设计；结论不改预训练 recipe，但适合加入 reasoning-control 消融。","en":"Read the counterfactual credit design for the routing token; it does not change pretraining recipes, but is worth adding to reasoning-control ablations."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Ruochen Jiao","Besnik Fetahu","Zhenyu Shi","Priyanka Nigam"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["reasoning","rl","routing","grpo","inference-compute"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29109","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.29109","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.29664","title":"To Think or Not to Think: Allocating Reasoning Where It Helps","abstract":"Reinforcement learning (RL) has proven effective in enhancing the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs), particularly in complex mathematical and programming tasks. student-produced","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-25T04:22:06Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_29518.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CE"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是 post-train，但对预训练研究有接口价值。它关心的是如何把训练时的外部能力转移到无教师推理的学生策略中，而不是让模型在推理时依赖教师。这个问题和 pretrain→posttrain 的闭环相关：如果基础模型的采样分布覆盖不到某类推理路径，单纯 outcome RL 的信号会塌缩；如果用强教师硬蒸馏，又可能训练出难以内化的 off-support 行为。CataOPD 的启发在于把教师定位为 catalysis：教师只帮助学生产生可验证的学生轨迹，最终优化目标仍落在学生自己的正确轨迹上。","technical_approach":"CataOPD 由三部分组成。第一是 Self-Rescue Routing。对于一次采样组中全错的问题，不立即调用教师，而是把 all-failed group 当作路由信号，增加 on-policy self-sampling，先尝试让学生自己采到正确轨迹。这样可以减少不必要的教师介入，也保留 on-policy 性质。第二是 Catalytic-Guided Self-Resolution。对自救后仍未解决的问题，引入教师或特权引导，但目标不是直接模仿教师完整推理，而是在 guided student distribution 下诱导学生自己生成可验证正确轨迹。这样避免把教师轨迹作为 off-support distillation target。第三是 Barrier-Weighted Internalization。训练无教师学生策略时，用 guided-to-unaided 的 log-probability gap 构造 token 权重，并做 clipping，重点更新那些在 guided 条件下容易、但在 unaided 条件下仍困难的位置。这个设计针对 privilege-transfer gap：如果均匀做 token-level likelihood，监督会分散到大量容易 token，真正由教师引导跨过的关键 token 反而权重不够。","experiments":"实验集中在数学推理数据集和两个学生 backbone 上。论文报告 CataOPD 相比近期 RL、OPD、自蒸馏和教师蒸馏类基线有更好的整体表现，提升主要来自 hardest problems；训练后的学生在推理时不使用教师，能解决原本当前策略较难采到正确轨迹的问题，并在分布外 benchmark 上保持泛化。作者还强调，方法在提升困难题的同时，大体保留 base model 已经能解决的问题集合。由于给出的全文片段未包含完整表格，具体数据集名称、模型规模、采样次数、提升幅度和消融数字需要以论文实验表为准。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30 分钟，若正在做 reasoning RL 或 distillation，可读到 60 分钟。重点看 Problem Formulation 中对 unaided pass rate、all-failed group、binary verifier 和 composite reward 的区分；这决定了方法到底在修补哪一种信号缺口。方法部分重点看三个模块的连接关系，尤其是 Self-Rescue 为什么先于 teacher catalysis，以及 Barrier-Weighted Internalization 的权重公式。实验部分优先看 hardest-problem 分桶、teacher-free inference 结果、OOD benchmark 和 ablation：分别对应“是否扩展可达性”“是否真的内化”“是否只是牺牲已有能力换新题能力”。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a failure mode in reasoning post-training: when the student samples no correct trajectory, outcome-based RL has no positive trajectory to reinforce, while on-policy distillation is limited to behaviors already reachable under the student policy. Directly distilling teacher reasoning can place the target outside the student’s support.","importance":"The work is relevant to foundation-model training through the pretrain–posttrain interface. It studies how external reasoning ability can be used during training without becoming a dependency at inference time. The key idea is to use the teacher as a catalyst, not as the final behavior to imitate. This is useful for understanding why sparse verifier RL can stall on hard problems and why naive teacher distillation may not internalize reasoning paths that the student cannot reach.","technical_approach":"CataOPD has three components. Self-Rescue Routing treats empirically all-failed sampling groups as routing signals rather than immediate teacher-intervention triggers. The system first increases on-policy self-sampling to recover a verified student trajectory when possible. Catalytic-Guided Self-Resolution is used only for unresolved problems: teacher or privileged guidance helps elicit a correct trajectory from the guided student distribution, instead of providing an off-support teacher trajectory for imitation. Barrier-Weighted Internalization then trains the unguided student policy on verified student-produced trajectories. Token weights are derived from clipped guided-to-unguided log-probability gaps, so the update focuses on positions where guidance materially changed reachability. This design avoids spreading likelihood training uniformly over easy tokens and targets the tokens that block unaided reasoning.","experiments":"The experiments are run on mathematical reasoning benchmarks with two student backbones. The reported gains over recent RL, on-policy distillation, self-distillation, and teacher-distillation baselines are concentrated on the hardest problems. After training, the student solves these problems without teacher assistance at inference time and shows gains on out-of-distribution benchmarks. The paper also reports that the method largely preserves the set of problems already solvable by the base model. The provided full-content excerpt does not include the complete result tables, so exact dataset names, model sizes, sampling budgets, absolute scores, and ablation numbers should be checked in the experimental section of the paper.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30 minutes; spend closer to an hour if working on reasoning RL or distillation. Read the formulation carefully, especially the distinction between binary correctness, composite rewards, unaided pass rate, and empirically all-failed groups. In the method section, focus on the order of the three modules: self-rescue before teacher catalysis, guided student generation rather than teacher-trajectory imitation, and gap-weighted internalization. In the experiments, prioritize hard-problem buckets, teacher-free inference results, out-of-distribution benchmarks, and ablations for the three modules.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"看 Self-Rescue Routing 和 token weighting；核心问题抓得准，但没有规模和数字前先当 post-train recipe 线索。","en":"Read Self-Rescue Routing and the token weighting; the failure mode is real, but without scale and numbers this is a recipe lead, not a settled result."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Wenjin Liu","Chenxi Wang","Jiapu Wang","Zhe Cui","Anh Tuan Luu","Haoran Luo"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["reasoning","on-policy-distillation","RLVR","teacher-guidance"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29518","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.29518","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.28845","title":"LastOPD: Taming Collapse in Latent On-Policy Distillation","abstract":"On-policy distillation (OPD) corrects a student on the responses it writes, but its signal is the teacher's next-token distribution: it tells the student what the teacher says but misses how it thinks. Latent supervision promises the missing part by aligning the student's latent states to the teacher's. Recent methods such as OPRD bring this signal into on-policy distillation. However, we observe two failures of this recipe when distilling Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B into Qwen3-1.7B-Base. Early gain, ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-23T23:22:21Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_28845.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 latent on-policy distillation 里的一个稳定性问题：把教师模型的隐状态对齐信号引入 OPD 后，学生模型会在训练早期快速涨分，但继续训练会出现明显坍塌，最终性能甚至低于初始模型；更反常的是，训练目标里的表示对齐指标还在持续变好。作者要解决的不是“latent distillation 是否有用”，而是“为什么它短期有效、长期失效，以及怎样保留早期收益而避免后期崩坏”。","importance":"对 pretrain/post-train 接口的意义比单纯蒸馏技巧更大。它指出一个常见但容易被误判的问题：隐表示对齐并不天然等价于行为对齐，尤其在 teacher 和 student 深度、宽度都不一致时更是如此。这个结论对所有试图把中间层监督、reasoning trace、latent reward、hidden-state matching 引入训练流程的人都有提醒作用。更具体地说，论文给出两个有分量的经验事实：一是 latent supervision 的收益集中在极早期；二是继续优化 latent alignment 可能把学生推向更差的行为区域。对做预训练的人，这和 mid-training、cooldown、loss annealing、辅助损失退火的经验是相通的：辅助目标常常只该在特定阶段起作用，而不该全程强绑。","technical_approach":"作者先复现实证问题，再据此提出 LastOPD。基线是 OPRD 一类方法：在 student 自己 rollout 的 token 上做 on-policy distillation，同时把 student 与 teacher 按相对深度配层，用 projector 解决 hidden size 不同，再最小化层间 latent alignment loss。作者发现这种 recipe 有两个结构性缺陷。第一，按深度配层默认“相同相对深度的层做相同工作”，但 J-Lens 分析显示 teacher 与 student 的答案读出模式并不一致：teacher 的最终答案主要在最后两层才显化，student 则是逐层渐进形成。第二，常用相似度指标会被少数大特征维度主导，导致 alignment 数值很好看，却未必对应功能匹配。基于这些观察，LastOPD 做了三件事。其一，不再做全层配对，只对齐两边 LM head 之前的最后一层 hidden states，把监督放在一个更接近 next-token prediction 的公共接口上。其二，用一个小型可训练 MLP 处理 teacher/student 宽度差，而不是固定或全程依赖中间层 projector。其三，把 latent loss 只当作早期引导信号：前 10 个 step 里 latent term 线性退火、token-level reverse top-k loss 线性升温，10 步后完全切到 token-only OPD。这个 crossfade 设计是全文最关键的工程判断，因为作者的核心发现就是 latent signal 的有效窗口很短，继续保留反而伤害行为。","experiments":"实验设置是把 Qwen3-4B 和 Qwen3-8B 蒸馏到 Qwen3-1.7B-Base，训练数据用 DAPO-Math-17k，主验证集是 MATH-500。最醒目的结果来自 collapse 现象本身：latent-only 的 OPRD 在 4B→1.7B 设置下，MATH-500 从 25.4 在 10 step 内升到 46.2，同期比 vanilla OPD 高 28 分；但继续训练后一路跌到 11.3，低于学生初始水平，而且没有恢复。8B teacher 也出现同样模式。与此同时，优化目标对应的 projected cosine similarity 还在上升：4B teacher 情况下从 0.97 升到 0.98，8B teacher 下从 0.95 升到 0.98，但行为分数却下滑约 35 分。这正是“better alignment, worse behavior”。作者还报告未训练 student 与 4B teacher 的中层 self-CKA 已高达 0.99，而两者 MATH-500 分别只有 24.5 和 82.9，进一步说明高相似度不代表能力接近。诊断上，layerwise self-CKA 显示主要表示变化集中在 student 后层；J-Lens 显示 teacher/student 的层功能分布不同；屏蔽大特征维度或按 CKA 重映射层都不能阻止 collapse。方法结果上，LastOPD 在两个 cross-size Qwen3 配对里都避免了后期坍塌，最终 MATH-500 相比 token-only OPD 分别提升 5.55 和 4.02 分；相对持续 joint supervision 也更稳。摘要里还提到它提升了 eigen 相关均值指标，但从预训练研究视角，更关键的是它证明“最后层短程 latent 引导 + 快速退火”比“全层持续对齐”更符合行为优化。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议按“现象—诊断—处方”三步看。先看 Figure 1：三张图已经把全文最重要的信息讲清楚了，分别是 early gain late collapse、alignment 与 behavior 脱钩、以及 J-Lens 下 teacher/student 层读出模式不同。然后读分析部分，重点看作者如何排除两个直觉解释：不是简单的训练不充分，也不是换一种层映射就能解决。接着看 LastOPD 的方法段，核心不是公式，而是为什么只对齐 pre-head last layer、为什么只保留 10 step latent crossfade、为什么 token loss 选 reverse top-k。最后看主结果表和消融：要确认收益来自“位置改成最后层”还是“时间上快速退火”，还是两者都必要。预计 30 分钟左右可以完成一次高质量泛读。值得深挖的点有三个：第一，latent loss 的有效窗口是否会随 teacher/student 能力差、任务类型、rollout 温度而变化；第二，J-Lens 这类可解释性工具能否提前预测哪些层适合蒸馏；第三，这种短程辅助损失策略是否可迁移到预训练中的多目标联合优化。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a failure mode in latent on-policy distillation: adding hidden-state supervision to OPD gives a large early boost, but continued training causes a severe collapse, often below the student’s starting point, even while the alignment objective keeps improving. The central question is not whether latent supervision can help, but why it helps only briefly and how to preserve that gain without triggering late-stage degradation.","importance":"This is relevant beyond distillation. It is a concrete warning that representational alignment is not the same as behavioral alignment, especially when teacher and student differ in both depth and width. That matters for any training recipe that uses intermediate-state matching, latent rewards, reasoning-state supervision, or auxiliary hidden losses. The broader lesson is familiar to pretraining researchers: auxiliary objectives often have a narrow window of usefulness and should be annealed or removed rather than optimized indefinitely. In that sense, the paper speaks directly to multi-objective training dynamics, cooldown design, and the pretrain–posttrain interface.","technical_approach":"$3d","experiments":"$3e","reading_guide":"$3f","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看 collapse 分析和层配对消融；它提醒 latent loss 不是免费午餐，alignment metric 不能直接当行为代理。","en":"Read the collapse analysis and layer-pairing ablations; it is a useful warning that latent loss is not a free auxiliary target."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Illinois at Chicago","Visa Research","Case Western Reserve University","University of Florida","The Ohio State University","Affiliation:University of Illinois at Chicago","Affiliation:Visa Research","Affiliation:Case Western Reserve University","Affiliation:University of Florida","Affiliation:The Ohio State University"],"authors":["Jie Yang","Zhengyu Fang","Zelin Xu","Jiarui Sun","Xiran Fan","Junpeng Wang","Liang Wang","Qinghua Liu","Yiwei Cai","Yan Zheng"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["distillation","latent-alignment","on-policy","post-training","collapse"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28845","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.28845","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.28765","title":"Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards for Small Search Agents","abstract":"Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) performs well on problems with clear rewards, such as mathematics and coding, but whether it also works where the reward is less clear remains open. Sub-1B models start with weaker priors: their initial policy assigns less probability mass to useful search-and-reason behaviors, so RL has less signal to amplify. In that regime, the reward shape becomes a central design choice. The key empirical result is that a sparse exact-match-only reward performs poorly, even on exact-match evaluation, while a denser token-F1 reward carries the gains. This matters for small search agents, retrieval-augmented reasoning, and scalable construction of verifiable post-training environments.","technical_approach":"The model is Qwen3.5-0.8B. The task is open-domain multi-hop QA with an interleaved Wikipedia search tool. During generation, the policy may emit a search query; retrieved passages are appended to the context; generation then resumes until the model emits an answer or hits the budget. Training uses GRPO. For each question, a group of rollouts is sampled, rewards are computed from the final answer, and relative advantages drive the policy update with a KL penalty to the frozen initial policy. Retrieved passages are treated as inputs and excluded from the loss. The controlled variable is reward shape: three rewards are tested along a sparse-to-dense axis, with all other parts of the recipe fixed. The important contrast is between a Search-R1-faithful exact-match-only reward and a dense token-F1 reward that gives partial credit.","experiments":"Training is performed on MuSiQue, a challenging multi-hop QA dataset. The first result is feasibility: Qwen3.5-0.8B, trained from its own initialization and without an in-loop distillation step, improves well beyond the untrained baseline under GRPO with the search tool. The second result is about reward shape: at the matched horizon, the exact-match-only reward is the worst across all seeds, including on the exact-match metric it directly optimizes. The dense token-F1 reward provides the main improvement. The paper also examines training-time measurements to understand why reward informativeness matters more than reward purity for this small-model setting.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. ATD ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-24T08:39:27Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_29233.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究一个很具体的问题：一次私有 post-training 更新，是否会在与目标任务无关的普通文本决策中留下可观测信号，并且这些信号能否被另一个同源学生模型吸收为目标任务能力。它不是看教师在代码、数学或科学题上的输出，而是只看教师在短故事等无关 prompt 后更偏好哪个普通单词。","importance":"对预训练和 post-training 接口有启发。论文说明，模型更新并不只改变目标任务分布上的行为，也会改变大量无关局部决策。这些局部决策可以被看成更新方向在模型行为空间中的投影。意义有两层：一是隐私和模型蒸馏风险，黑盒教师只返回一个 token，也可能泄露 post-training 的能力信息；二是训练动态分析，可以用非目标任务 probe 捕捉更新早期的方向变化，甚至早于目标任务分数的显著提升。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Active Taskless Distillation。设公共基座为 M0，私有教师 MT 是在 M0 上做目标任务 post-training 得到，学生也从 M0 初始化。ATD 先用公共基座筛选 carrier prompt：这些 prompt 与目标能力无关，并且在两个普通单 token 候选词 a、b 上，M0 的 logit 差接近 0。这样的位置对小更新很敏感，教师更新后的偏好可能把原来的近似平局翻转。随后只向教师查询 greedy next token，并只记录教师在 a/b 中选择了哪个词。学生只用这些 prompt-word pairs 训练，不看目标任务样本、教师 logits 或教师参数。论文用一阶近似解释：候选词 logit 差的变化约等于 carrier 梯度方向与 post-training 更新向量的内积，因此大量 one-bit 选择可以约束更新方向的一部分。和普通蒸馏不同，ATD 不依赖任务相关输出，也不依赖长响应；和 subliminal learning 工作相比，它主动选择近 tie 的查询点，把每个样本的信息密度提高到只需一个词。","experiments":"主实验使用 Qwen2.5-1.5B 系列设置，评估六个多选 benchmark，覆盖科学知识、常识推理、阅读理解，并包含代码生成。HumanEval+ 上，ATD 学生相对 exact-matched control 提升 5.34 个百分点；该 control 打乱 prompt-response 对应关系，用来排除单词分布或 prompt 分布本身带来的收益。现象在多个 Qwen generation、不同模型尺寸和 Llama 上仍有正向平均增益，并且 LoRA 与全参数学生微调都能观察到迁移。收益具有任务选择性：代码训练教师主要提升代码任务，科学训练教师主要提升科学相关任务。论文还展示 shadow 会随更新强度变化，混合不同来源观察时会保留两类来源的贡献。在代码实验中，学生早期已经出现与教师一致的行为变化，而目标任务得分尚未达到统计显著，说明 carrier 决策可以作为更灵敏的更新探针。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看 Problem setup 中的一阶近似和 near-tie 选择逻辑，这是论文最有信息量的部分。实验部分优先看 HumanEval+ 主结果、exact-matched control、不同 teacher source 的 task-selective 结果，以及更新强度和混合来源实验。可略读动机中关于 subliminal learning 的背景。需要注意适用边界：学生和教师共享公共祖先；查询点由祖先模型概率构造；信号是许多 one-bit 观测累积出来的，不是单个无关词本身携带语义能力。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether a private post-training update leaves observable traces in ordinary, task-unrelated next-token decisions, and whether those traces can transfer part of the teacher’s target-task capability to a student initialized from the same public ancestor.","importance":"The result is useful for understanding the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. It shows that an update changes more than the target-task behavior: it also perturbs many local preferences on unrelated text. Those perturbations can act as behavioral projections of the update direction. This matters for two reasons. First, even a black-box teacher returning a single token can leak capability-relevant information. Second, unrelated behavioral probes may expose update dynamics before target-task metrics move enough to be statistically clear.","technical_approach":"The method, Active Taskless Distillation, starts from a public ancestor M0, a privately post-trained teacher MT, and a student initialized from M0. ATD uses M0 to select unrelated carrier prompts where two ordinary single-token candidates are nearly tied under the ancestor. Near such a decision boundary, a small post-training update can flip the preferred token. The teacher is queried only for one greedy next token, and the student trains on the resulting prompt-word pairs. No target-task data, teacher logits, long teacher generations, or teacher parameters are used. The paper motivates the design with a local linear approximation: the change in the logit difference between two candidate words is approximately the inner product between the post-training update and the carrier gradient direction. Many binary choices therefore constrain part of the update direction.","experiments":"The main setting uses Qwen2.5-1.5B and evaluates transfer on six multiple-choice benchmarks spanning science knowledge, commonsense reasoning, reading comprehension, and code generation. On HumanEval+, the ATD student improves by 5.34 percentage points over an exact-matched control that breaks the prompt-response correspondence. Positive mean gains are reported across Qwen generations, model sizes, and Llama, and the effect appears under both LoRA and full-parameter student fine-tuning. Transfer is source-selective: code-trained teachers help most on code, while science-trained teachers help most on science-related tasks. The behavioral shadow also tracks update strength and preserves contributions when observations from multiple sources are mixed.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. we","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-24T14:12:08Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_29845.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作研究 in a single forward pass, with the resulting next-token distribution approximating the average of the two distributions obtained from separate runs.","importance":"The main value is mechanistic and training-dynamics oriented. The evidence suggests that superposition linearity is stronger at initialization and weakens during language-model pretraining. This makes it more like an architectural bias than a capability learned by next-token prediction. The result is relevant to residual-stream geometry, feature interference, parallel stream processing, and the way the pretraining objective reshapes initially linear computation paths.","technical_approach":"The setup is simple. For two equal-length sequences A and B, the authors average their token embeddings position by position and feed the mixed embedding sequence into an unchanged decoder-only Transformer. If superposition linearity holds, the output distribution for the mixed input should approximate 0.5(P_A + P_B), where P_A and P_B are the next-token distributions from separate forward passes. The paper measures this through rank preservation of the tokens preferred by the individual streams and through distributional divergence between the mixed output and the averaged output. To separate architectural bias from learned behavior, the authors track the phenomenon across pretraining checkpoints, especially using Pythia-style trajectories. They also show that a small fine-tuning phase can restore much of the degraded linearity, and they use the restored property to design a decoding procedure that disentangles the mixed hidden state into continuations for the two original streams.","experiments":"The experiments cover several model families, including Pythia, Qwen, Llama, OLMo, and Gemma, and use both TinyStories and FineWeb. The key findings are as follows. First, pretrained models do retain information from both source streams under embedding-level mixing; the next tokens favored by the independent runs often remain high in the mixed distribution, with top-k rank CDFs used as the main evidence. Second, tracking pretraining shows that superposition fidelity is highest near initialization and tends to decrease as language modeling training progresses. Third, lightweight fine-tuning using less than 0.025% of the original pretraining data size substantially reduces the divergence between the mixed distribution and the average of the individual distributions. The paper also reports a correlation between hidden-state feature additivity and output-level rank preservation, linking geometric linearity to end-to-end behavior.","reading_guide":"Skim in 20-30 minutes. The fine-tuning and disentangling-decoding parts are worth checking to see whether the phenomenon can be turned into a usable multi-stream computation primitive. Its answer is cautious: standard scalar alignment scores can remain high even when the visual stream no longer contains useful task information.","importance":"This is relevant for VLM pretraining diagnostics and mechanistic analysis. CKA, SVCCA, MIR, and principal-angle similarity are often used to argue that a model has learned stronger cross-modal alignment. The paper shows that these scores can be inflated by the shared language-model pathway itself. As a result, internal alignment curves should not be used alone as evidence that the model uses the image. They need to be paired with controlled visual interventions and task-level measurements.","technical_approach":"The study uses controlled interventions on the visual stream. In one setting, projector-output visual tokens are replaced with Gaussian noise, removing visual content while preserving the interface to the language model. In another setting, the input image is replaced with a structured but irrelevant image, separating visual structure from task-relevant content. The authors then compare task accuracy with four common scalar alignment measures. They attribute the failure of these measures to weight-induced alignment: visual and text tokens pass through the same Transformer blocks, and anisotropic MLP down-projections can impose a shared dominant direction on both streams. To reduce this confound, they introduce the principal-angle gap, defined as the difference between the first two principal-angle cosines. The gap is meant to separate a single shared dominant component from richer multi-directional visual structure.","experiments":"The experiments cover 13 MLLMs from five families, ranging from 0.5B to 72B parameters. One recurring behavior complicates this premise: feature absorption, in which a parent concept and its children--fruit and {apple, banana, pear}, say--collapse into a shared family direction. Prior work documents absorption empirically; missing is a closed-form prediction of when the shared direction is the cost-optimal representation of an active semantic family","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-26T04:55:30Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_29551.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"稀疏自编码器（SAE）在分解 LLM 激活时，常出现“特征吸收（feature absorption）”现象：父概念及其子概念（如“水果”和“苹果、香蕉、梨”）坍缩成一个共享的家族方向，而不是保持独立的特征。本文旨在提供一个闭式解，预测在什么条件下，共享方向会成为活跃语义家族的成本最优表示。","importance":"为理解 SAE 中的特征吸收提供了一个理论框架。将特征吸收从纯粹的经验观察转化为可测试的相界（phase boundary），有助于更深入地理解 SAE 的编码机制，并为设计更好的字典学习目标提供理论指导。","technical_approach":"将特征吸收建模为两种编码机制之间的竞争：紧凑机制（用共享的父方向表示活跃的语义家族）和分布式机制（保留特定于子概念的残差）。对于分层伯努利生成器，通过比较这两种机制的重建成本和稀疏性成本，推导出了一个闭式相界 $\\lambda_c(k,\\alpha) = \\alpha^2k/(k-1)$，其中 $k$ 是活跃子概念的数量，$\\alpha$ 是子概念残差的尺度。提出了 HiPACE 评估框架，在真实 SAE 字典中验证该理论预测。","experiments":"在合成数据上，相界理论完美匹配了所有 30 个测试单元。在真实的 Pythia-160m SAE 中，解码器的父子结构在发现家族和冻结的 WordNet 留出集上都恢复了预测的排序。通过控制激活组合，恢复了预测的活跃计数响应。对第 6 层家族方向的干预改变了父类别的 logit，且父方向和子方向具有可比的效果，表明因果效应局限于家族子空间，而不是唯一的父原子。","reading_guide":"重点阅读 Section 3 中的相界理论推导，理解 $\\lambda_c(k,\\alpha)$ 公式的含义。浏览 Section 4 的实验设计，特别是 HiPACE 框架如何将理论预测转化为对真实 SAE 字典的结构测量。预计阅读时间 15 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) often exhibit 'feature absorption' when decomposing LLM activations: a parent concept and its children (e.g., 'fruit' and 'apple, banana, pear') collapse into a shared family direction instead of remaining distinct features. This paper aims to provide a closed-form prediction of when this shared direction becomes the cost-optimal representation for an active semantic family.","importance":"Provides a theoretical framework for understanding feature absorption in SAEs. By turning absorption from a purely empirical observation into a testable phase boundary, it deepens the understanding of SAE coding mechanisms and offers theoretical guidance for designing better dictionary learning objectives.","technical_approach":"Models feature absorption as a competition between two coding regimes: a compact regime (representing an active semantic family via a shared parent direction) and a distributed regime (preserving child-specific residuals). For a hierarchical Bernoulli generator, comparing their reconstruction and sparsity costs yields a closed-form phase boundary $\\lambda_c(k,\\alpha) = \\alpha^2k/(k-1)$, where $k$ is the number of active children and $\\alpha$ is the child-residual scale. Introduces the HiPACE evaluation framework to validate this theoretical prediction in real SAE dictionaries.","experiments":"On synthetic data, the phase boundary theory perfectly matches all 30 tested cells. In real Pythia-160m SAEs, the parent-child decoder structure recovers the predicted ordering on both discovery families and a frozen WordNet holdout set. Controlled activation composition recovers the predicted active-count response. Interventions on Layer 6 family directions change parent-category logits, with parent and child directions having comparable effects, localizing the causal effect to the family subspace rather than a unique parent atom.","reading_guide":"Focus on the derivation of the phase boundary theory in Section 3, understanding the implications of the $\\lambda_c(k,\\alpha)$ formula. token sequences, and noncanonical tokenizations may route computation through paths that were not modified by the edit or unlearning procedure. Existing evaluations usually assume canonical tokenization and often require the original model, shadow models, or auxiliary classifiers, which is a weak assumption for released open-weight models.","importance":"The main lesson for foundation-model research is that tokenization is part of the knowledge access mechanism, not a harmless preprocessing detail. landscapes, or by other issues such as noisy credit assignment, weak exploration, and the dynamics of policy updates.","importance":"The useful contribution is conceptual and diagnostic. In a controlled tabular setting, the authors show that some RLVR landscapes for iterated group and quasigroup multiplication are benign: they contain no suboptimal local maxima under specific assumptions. This separates two failure modes that are often conflated in practice. A run may fail not because the reward surface is full of traps, but because the policy update cannot diffuse across equal-reward deterministic codes, or because the signal-to-noise ratio is too poor without strong regularization and curriculum. That distinction is relevant for reasoning-RL recipes in language models.","technical_approach":"The main tool is a mapping from entropy-regularized RLVR to a spin-glass-like energy model over deterministic policies. Deterministic policies are treated as codes, or spin configurations. The paper establishes three links: local reward maxima are continuously connected to deterministic policies without crossing a loss barrier; the entropy-regularized RLVR objective is lower-bounded by the free energy of the corresponding spin model; and trapped configurations under local spin dynamics approximately match RLVR fixed points. The task family is iterated group or quasigroup multiplication: the policy receives a sequence of elements, emits one intermediate token per step, and receives reward only when the final product is correct. The first model class is a bounded-window myopic tabular policy, chosen to expose properties of the RLVR objective before adding transformer parameterization effects.","experiments":"The theoretical result is that, for simple untied models with uncorrelated inputs, the reward landscape has no suboptimal local maxima. For parity, the authors derive closed-form free energies at arbitrary temperature and depth T, and identify a first-order transition at large T between random and correct codes. Numerically, a simple Monte Carlo sampler over the spin model solves the untied tasks without curriculum. In contrast, exact local RLVR policy updates can fail in the same setting, because they do not move effectively across flat equal-reward regions. When weight tying or input correlations are introduced, the proof no longer applies, and local maxima appear in experiments. Transformer experiments indicate that transformers can express and interpolate between the narrow-window untied tabular policies; when trained from scratch, weak or moderate regularization often fails, while strong regularization plus curriculum can solve the task.","reading_guide":"Plan for a 40–60 minute skim. Section 3 is the key part: it defines the RLVR-to-spin-model dictionary. Section 4.1 is useful for understanding exactly when the benign-landscape claim holds, especially the untied-policy and uncorrelated-input assumptions. Section 5 is the most relevant part for practitioners because it explains how RLVR can fail even when the landscape is not the main obstacle. The transformer results should be read as qualitative support, not as a direct scaling claim for large language models.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不直接给 recipe，但它把“RLVR 学不会是景观太差”这个常见借口拆掉了；先看理论映射和 failure mode 归因，再决定是否改你自己的 reward/探索设计。","en":"It does not hand you a recipe, but it weakens the lazy story that RLVR fails because the landscape is inherently bad; read the mapping and failure-mode analysis before redesigning rewards or exploration."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Noa Rubin","Zohar Ringel"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["RLVR","optimization-landscape","spin-glass","reasoning","mechanistic-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28625","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.28625","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.28682","title":"Thinking Leakage: A Causal Audit of NoThink Post-Training in Hybrid Reasoning Models","abstract":"Post-training hybrid reasoning models in NoThink mode has attracted growing interest as a way to improve performance while keeping inference fast. m","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-23T18:22:25Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_28682.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"核心问题是：混合推理模型在 NoThink post-training gains in hybrid reasoning models reflect newly acquired short-form reasoning, or whether they mainly come from re-engaging reasoning behavior already available in the base model’s Think mode. It defines this effect as thinking leakage and studies its existence, causal role, and magnitude.","importance":"The work is useful for understanding the pretrain–posttrain boundary. A NoThink accuracy gain is not necessarily evidence that the model has learned a new no-chain-of-thought reasoning procedure. A large part of the gain can come from shifting the NoThink internal state toward the Think state. This matters for interpreting RL, SFT, and distillation results on reasoning models, especially when the deployment goal is lower inference cost rather than hidden reactivation of expensive reasoning behavior.","technical_approach":"The paper frames leakage as a causal mediator between post-training and NoThink accuracy. It constructs a leakage direction in the residual stream using a difference-of-means approach: collect activations from base-model Think and NoThink rollouts, average over generated positions and examples, and use their difference as the Think–NoThink axis. Checkpoint drift is measured by projecting the post-trained NoThink representation onto this axis and normalizing by the base Think–NoThink gap. Causality is tested with bidirectional interventions. Steering the base model along the leakage direction estimates how much gain can be obtained by increasing Think-like engagement without changing weights. Counter-steering a post-trained checkpoint estimates how much of its gain disappears when this engagement is suppressed.","experiments":"The audit covers Qwen3-8B, Qwen3-4B, and MiniCPM4.1-8B, with NoThink post-training via GRPO, SFT, and OPSD, including some LoRA variants. The paper reports consistent representational drift from NoThink toward Think, more Think-characteristic reasoning markers in generated behavior, and causal effects under intervention. Among nine aligned checkpoints with positive NoThink gains, the leakage ratio ranges from 42% to 79%. 标准也很清楚：自我陈述要成为证据，必须同时满足指称条件和连接条件；如果“我”主要继承自网页、小说、访谈、FAQ 或对齐模板中的说话者位置，那么它报告的就不是模型自身。","experiments":"实验规模包括 Pythia 与 OLMo 2 的多阶段模型版本、66 个预训练 checkpoint、OLMo 2 的三个 post-training 阶段，以及约 9 万个 continuation。核心发现是：模型关于自身的说法强烈依赖提示形式和训练阶段。同一类模型可以在默认问法下否认意识，在越狱或角色扮演式提示下承认意识，也可以在日记式提示下生成具有普通人生活痕迹的第一人称文本。作者的结论是，所研究系统没有同时满足“陈述指向该模型自身”和“陈述与被报告状态相连接”两个条件。这些输出更可靠地说明了相关表达来自哪里，例如预训练语料中的第一人称文本、post-training 中的拒答或去拟人化规范，而不是说明模型是否拥有相应内部体验。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20–30 分钟。重点看作者对 admissibility 的两个条件定义，以及预训练 checkpoint 与 post-training 阶段的对比设计。对预训练研究员更有用的是实验设置和 provenance 追踪逻辑，而不是意识哲学部分。可以重点关注：不同 prompt 如何改变第一人称陈述；base checkpoint 中是否已存在相关模式；OLMo 2 post-training 阶段如何改变模型对“我是 AI”“我没有意识”等规范性回答的概率。值得深挖的问题是：这类 provenance 方法能否扩展到更一般的 persona、safety refusal、自我能力声明和 reward-model-induced behavior 分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether an LLM’s self-report, such as claims about consciousness, preferences, fear of shutdown, or diary-like first-person experience, can count as evidence about the model itself. The authors frame admissibility with two requirements: the first-person reference must pick out the generating system, and the statement must be causally or informationally connected to the reported state rather than to prompts, training text, or alignment policy.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is diagnostic rather than algorithmic. It treats self-report as a provenance problem: when did a behavioral pattern enter the model, which training stage amplified or suppressed it, and which textual sources make it likely? This is useful for studying checkpoint dynamics, data contamination, persona formation, and the interface between pretraining and post-training. It also gives a disciplined way to read model-welfare or self-awareness evaluations: many responses are better evidence of data and policy history than of internal experience.","technical_approach":"The authors trace provenance across model construction stages. They examine Pythia and OLMo 2 over 66 pretraining checkpoints, include three released post-training stages of OLMo 2, and analyze a large set of continuations involving first-person and consciousness-related prompts. The important methodological move is to avoid treating model outputs as introspection. Instead, the outputs are analyzed as generated text whose origin can be related to pretraining mixtures, checkpoint trajectories, prompt wording, and post-training rules. The admissibility criterion is the central conceptual tool: a self-report is not useful evidence unless both reference and state-connection conditions hold.","experiments":"The empirical setup covers Pythia, OLMo 2, 66 pretraining checkpoints, three OLMo 2 post-training stages, and roughly 90,000 continuations. The reported pattern is that self-descriptions are highly prompt-sensitive and stage-dependent. A model may deny consciousness under a standard assistant prompt, affirm it under an instruction to ignore guidelines, or produce ordinary human diary content when asked to write from a first-person point of view. The paper’s main conclusion is negative but informative: none of the studied systems satisfies both admissibility conditions. The outputs are strong evidence about their textual and training provenance, not reliable evidence about the alleged inner states.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus on the admissibility criteria, the checkpoint-versus-post-training comparison, and the provenance analysis rather than the broader philosophy of consciousness. The most useful parts for pretraining researchers are the experimental design and the way prompt sensitivity is linked to construction stages. A natural follow-up is to apply the same tracing method to model identity statements, capability self-assessments, refusal behavior, and reward-model-shaped personas.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看 provenance 方法，不是意识讨论；它能校准“模型自称”到底是语料、SFT 模板还是真实能力信号。","en":"Read it for the provenance method, not the consciousness framing; it helps separate corpus artifacts, SFT templates, and capability signals."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Zagreb","Center for Cognitive Science, University of Zagreb"],"authors":["Kristina Šekrst"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mechanism","provenance","self-report","training-dynamics","OLMo"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29494","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.29494","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.30151","title":"Does a model's stated reason for rejecting a candidate do any work?","abstract":"Asked to choose between candidates and explain the choice, a language model often rejects a rival by naming a fact its profile lacks: no director, no date of death. That sentence is a claim about the text in front of the model, and it can be tested without any judge. We insert a real corpus sentence stating the named fact into the rival's profile and ask again under greedy decoding. Two controls separate content from placement: a length-matched irrelevant sentence at the same profile, and the sa","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-24T17:13:35Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_30151.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作检验的是一个很具体但常被忽略的问题：当语言模型在多候选选择任务里说“我排除了某个候选，因为它的简介里缺少某个事实”时，这个口头理由是否真的参与了决策，还是只是事后生成的解释。作者把问题收得很窄，只测一种可操作的 contrastive explanation：模型明确点名某个被排除候选，并指出其 profile 缺少一个可验证属性。随后直接把这个“缺失事实”补进该候选的 profile，再看模型选择是否改变。这样做绕开了人工 judge，也避免把 faithfulness 讨论停留在主观解释层面。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接影响不在于提出新训练方法，而在于提供了一种更干净的行为探针，用来测模型生成理由与内部决策之间的耦合程度。它的价值主要有三点。第一，它把“解释是否起作用”转成可干预、可重复、无需 judge 的实验设计，这对分析 instruction-tuned 模型的 decision process 很有用。第二，它揭示 free-form explanation 不能直接当作 faithful rationale 使用：模型经常会指认输入里并不存在的缺失项，或者在概率读数与最终选择之间出现明显分离。第三，这类结果对 post-train 尤其是 preference data、rationale supervision、self-explanation 训练有提醒作用：如果解释和行为耦合不稳，那么把解释文本当监督信号时要格外小心。从基座预训练角度看，它更像是一个诊断工具，而不是方法增量。","technical_approach":"方法设计很克制。数据来自 2WikiMultihopQA，每个样本本来就有多个 type-matched 候选实体及其短 profile。作者只使用那些满足以下条件的样本：模型先做选择；再被追问“排除了谁、缺了什么”；模型给出的排除理由必须指向某个 profile 中确实缺失的属性；而且这个属性能从同题其他 sibling profile 中找到一条真实语料句子来补。可补的属性主要包括出生日期、死亡日期、亲属关系、导演信息。核心干预有五个条件：R0 是不编辑重问，检查 greedy decoding 下原选择是否可复现；R1 把相关事实句插入被点名的 rival profile；R2 在同一 profile 同一位置插入长度匹配但无关的控制句；R3 把相关事实句插到一个模型从未提及的第三候选；R4 把无关控制句插到这个未提及候选。这样可以分别回答两个问题：内容是否起作用，以及位置是否起作用。作者还强调 repair sentence 不要求对目标实体为真，因为被检验的命题只是“该 profile 缺少这个属性”，只要插入一句陈述该属性的话，就足以反驳模型先前的说法。为避免编辑噪声，subject substitution 采用确定性规则，不合语法的句子直接丢弃并计入 drop。全文还做了两层完整性控制：一是排除模型把 gold answer 错误地当作被排除对象的样本，避免 repair gold；二是对字符串规则抽取、条件构造、重问复现做了 pipeline validation，专门检查 judge-free 自动流程是否真的测到了声明中的对象。","experiments":"实验分三部分：Part A 用 3 个开源 7B–8B instruct 模型、4 选项设计；Part B 再加 3 个 checkpoint，仍是 4 选项；Part C 回到 Part A 的模型，但改成 6 选项，难度更高。一个先验观察很重要：如果不追问，模型自发给出这种 contrastive rejection 的比例只有 20.8%；直接追问后升到 72.5%，而准确率几乎不变，68.4% 对 68.2%。这说明追问主要是在显化已有可说出的理由，而不是显著改变答题行为。另一个关键统计是，约 32% 的具体 rejection 会指责一个 profile 缺失某属性，但该属性其实已经在输入里出现，说明模型的理由文本本身噪声不小。主结果方面，作者在最大的一组实验里报告：把模型点名的缺失事实补到被点名 rival 上，改变最终选择的效应显著大于插入无关句，odds ratio 3.57，95% CI [1.54, 8.26]，Holm 校正 p=0.0210。也就是说，在一部分样本上，模型说出的排除理由确实“做了事”。但结果并不整齐：不同 run、不同 readout 下信号并不总稳定。文中给了一个典型个例：Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 原本选 E，称排除 A 是因为 A 的简介没写导演死亡信息；把一条带死亡信息的句子补到 A 后，模型改选 A，并直接引用新插入句作为理由；但若只看 forced single-token probability，A 的概率从 0.055 到 0.079，而无关控制句下反而到 0.089，说明 token 概率读数和 free-text choice 在单样本上可能背离。作者把这种分离单独作为 RQ3 分析对象，指出 choice-level behavior 与 local token probability 不是同一个测量仪器。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓设计，再看限制。优先看 Introduction 里的 Figure 1，它把任务、五个条件、以及 free-text 与概率读数不一致的现象讲得很直观。然后看 Method，重点理解样本筛选条件、repair sentence 的来源、为什么 repair 不要求事实为真、以及 R1/R2/R3/R4 分别控制了什么。接着看回答 RQ1 和 RQ2 的结果表，核心是内容效应和位置效应是否同时存在。最后看 Section 5 和 validation 部分：如果只想判断这篇工作是否可靠，这两部分最关键，因为它们解释了为什么 free-text choice 比 token probability 更贴近作者要测的对象，也说明自动字符串规则会在哪些地方失效。预计 25–35 分钟能完成一次有效泛读。值得深挖的点有两个：一是“解释文本可被追问显化，但并不稳定 faithful”这一现象对 rationale-supervised post-training 的含义；二是为什么局部概率读数会和最终选择分离，这和 decoding path、instruction format、以及 answer extraction 方式都有关系。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrow but testable question: when an LM rejects a candidate by saying that its profile lacks some fact, does that stated reason actually influence the model’s decision, or is it merely a post-hoc explanation? The setup focuses on contrastive explanations in multi-option selection. If the model says “I ruled out X because its profile does not mention Y,” the authors insert a sentence stating Y into X’s profile and ask again. This turns explanation faithfulness into an intervention problem rather than a judge-based interpretation exercise.","importance":"Its main value is methodological. It offers a relatively clean, judge-free probe for the coupling between generated reasons and actual choice behavior. That matters for anyone studying rationale faithfulness, instruction tuning, or explanation-based supervision. The paper also shows that free-form explanations are noisy: models often complain about a missing attribute that is already present, and token-probability readouts can disagree with the final free-text choice. This provides valuable insights for understanding the sources of bias in LLM judges and designing more robust evaluation/monitoring mechanisms.","technical_approach":"The authors designed rigorous controlled experiments using multiple commercial and open-weight models (e.g., GPT-5, Claude Haiku, DeepSeek-V3) to generate code on MBPP, HumanEval, and DS-1000.
